| C93    | resolved.trace: resolved sources, the requested window, the default price-basis reading (whether it applied and the StatVars it removed before bind), capability_version, conversion recipe, each slot_filters entry's own provenance (the binder's election, a code row recognised in the query's own text, a value the measure-family election bound on an axis the binder left open, or a value a code-index declared default (code_index.types.CodeSystemDeclaration.default) bound onto an open slot once the query bound another slot into that default's own scoping subtree, e.g. crsTypeOfFlow defaulting to CRSTypeOfFlow10 for an unqualified grants question, or a value a shape slot default (`CodeIndex.shape_slot_defaults`) bound onto an open slot of its population type: medicalStatus defaulting to LiveBirth on an elected BirthEvent shape; an alternate shape keeps the values its representative member declares), and query_qualifiers naming each narrowing term the request's text named, the code rows it names in the packaged index, the slot it bound or, for an unbound value the answer's shape has a slot for, that slot (if any), and its disposition (bound/unbound/no_vocabulary). A record may also carry its `start` and `end` offsets in the variable phrase, its `evidence` (label, synonym, model, uncovered, unavailable or unsupported), the `operation` (union or refinement) that combined its codes with another term's, and the codes it `selected` before that combination. Code matches on one code system bind by the connective between them (`phase_rank_bind._combine_matches`, `qualifier_match.joins`). Matches joined by union connectives (`qualifier_match.UNION_CONNECTIVES`) are a union. A code nesting under another of the union drops, and codes that do not nest bind a set, so "health and education" and "health & education" bind {120, 110}, "health including malaria control" binds 120, and "HIV" and "AIDS" in "HIV/AIDS and population policy, programme, and reproductive health" bind 130 and are bound through the union, which selected 13040 and 130. An ancestor binds a code only through a union. Two terms joined any other way, or by nothing, refine to the narrower code when one nests under the other ("malaria control within health" binds 12262) and otherwise leave the code system to the model's binding. An exclusion or comparison connective (`EXCLUSION_CONNECTIVES`, `COMPARISON_CONNECTIVES`) followed by a code match, across function words alone or, after "compare" or "compared", at any distance, makes the phrase from the connective to that match a no_vocabulary record with evidence `unsupported` ("excluding malaria control", "compare aid to health"), and codes it sets side by side form no union. The code an exclusion governs, and each code a union joins to it, takes no part in the binding, so "health excluding malaria control" binds 120 and the record of "malaria control" is unbound. A code system whose every code an exclusion removes is not forced. "ODA excluding health" keeps the model's binding, which the record of "health" judges. A comparison after an exclusion compares the code the exclusion kept. Only the variable's matches carry offsets. A code the query text or a dropped phrase names that the variable names too is read at the variable's offsets alone, so "health and education ODA" with the query text "health and education ODA to Kenya" still binds {120, 110}. A comparison of two codes of one system that do not nest refuses in either coverage mode. Every other such phrase refuses under `refuse` only, and under `log` is logged as a would-refuse line. Evidence `unavailable` marks a code term the elected shape cannot apply, meaning one naming only codes no shape can bind (`qualifier_match.bindable_codes`, where no index row holds the code or a descendant of it and index rows carry its code system, as with `CRSTypeOfFinance4`, "population"), whose words the shape's own reading leaves unread, or, on a part naming a dataset, one no row of that dataset holds (C78b). A shape's own reading matches, ties and forces only codes some shape can bind. A part naming a dataset refuses qualifier_not_bound on an unavailable record in either coverage mode, with the elected identity as flagged_match. A part naming none does so under `refuse`. Under `log` it logs the would-refuse line for the term and departs it `measure_not_applied` with the `query_qualifier_dropped` warning. Each run of variable words no reader covers beside a code span of the elected shape's reading or a phrase a menu choice reads, or joined to one by a union connective (`qualifier_match.UNION_CONNECTIVES`), is an unbound record with evidence `uncovered` naming that span's rows (`phase_rank_bind._coverage_records`), such as "maternal" in "maternal health ODA" beside sector 120, and "maternal" in "maternal and infant health ODA" joined to "infant health" when the model chooses 12240 for it. On an elected shape the measure index describes, one whose answer carries a value of the measure index's populationType axis, a word names a matchable measure value when every name of the value's reading holds it (the label and each synonym of the value and of each alias of it, `MeasureIndex.reading_set`), no span of the variable names the value, and the answer over the elected shape and the final bindings does not carry it (`phase_rank_bind._uncarried_measure_words`). A function word names no measure value, and neither does a word inside a span read in every code system together or a word of a name of a measure value the answer carries, matchable or not. On any other shape no word names a measure value. In a run beside no code span such a word is unread without a rating, on a shape with or without a named member, and each stretch of unread words holding one is an unbound record with evidence `uncovered` naming those measure values, such as "mobilised" in "private sector investment mobilised", which names amountMobilised ("amount mobilised", "private finance mobilised") on a financial-flow amount series, where "private" and "finance" name nothing. "inflation" names no RealValue, since "real terms" lacks it though the alias "inflation adjusted" holds it. A run beside a code span that holds such a word names the measure values beside the code rows, so "mobilised" in "health ODA mobilised" names CRSTypeOfFlow10 and amountMobilised. The run's other words are rated. The words of the measure a member's name reads are those every member the read served holds in its name (`phase_rank_bind.served_member_names`), and with no read served, as on a refusal before the read, those every named member of the shape holds. The binder rates, once per pass over the final bindings and before the part's answer is first judged (`phase_election.judge_coverage`), how likely the elected shape's series states each of the run's other words the readers leave unread, and each stretch of words it rates below `SearchSettings.stated_word_bar` (0.3) is a no_vocabulary record with evidence `uncovered` naming no rows, such as "refugee" in "refugee population" on the count of all persons and "tax" in "tax revenue as a percent of GDP" on total revenue. Where the names of the members the read served differ from the elected shape's label, several names or one other name, the rating reads the words on those series (`stated_words_read.serves_beyond_label`), and Jev asks every word again, since its bind rated them on the label: "refugee" is rated on "Total population" when a read under the label "Refugee population" serves that series alone. A word rated at the bar or above reads as stated. So does a word the binder leaves unrated or a read that fails, and the part then notes a degraded read, so the response cache stores no such answer. The record's flagged match is the elected series. Each candidate the part offers beside or in place of the elected answer is judged by the same rule on the members its own read served (`phase_election.judge_answer_coverage`), the binder rating the words the readers leave on it in one call per candidate, all at once, and none for a candidate the readers clear. A record is unmet only on the answers whose own reading records it (`requirements.judge`), so on "refugee assistance Kenya" with data in both directions the direction candidate "Assistance received" records "refugee" in its trace, the elected "Refugee assistance given" records nothing, and the part departs `query_qualifier_dropped` for the word. A run's neighbours lie past the amount words, data words and flow verbs beside it, so "maternal" in "maternal data ODA" and "water" in "water spending ODA" are records beside ODA. The readers are the spans of that reading, code and measure values, the terms already recorded, the part's entity text, matched as a code label is across the separators it holds and with either apostrophe ("China" in "Hong Kong SAR, China ODA" and "Ivoire" in "Côte d’Ivoire ODA" are read), a possessive ending included, date phrases, `function_words.FUNCTION_WORDS`, the amount words `function_words.AMOUNT_WORDS` ("spending", "expenditure", "funding", "financing", "total", "figures", "amount", "amounts", "money", "flows"), the nouns naming data itself `function_words.DATA_WORDS` ("data", "statistics", "numbers", "information", "indicators", "series", as with "data" in "health data"), a connective between two code spans, the camelCase words of the shape's five-tuple dcids and its population type name (`phase_rank_bind._series_words`), the words every label of the datasets the part answers from holds, the words those datasets state of the rows the part reads (`phase_rank_bind._dataset_stated_words`, C78b: "bilateral" on the CRS, and on DAC2a or DAC2b where the part's recipient is a country, and "multilateral" there where it names the provider the part leaves unnamed), the flow verbs `function_words.FLOW_VERBS` (received, receive, receives, receiving, given, provided, disbursed, spent, going, goes, went, reported, reports, as with "received" in "net ODA received by Kenya" and "reported" in "total ODA reported to the DAC"), on a shape with a measurement denominator the ratio words `function_words.RATIO_WORDS` (share, ratio, percent, percentage, proportion) and the words of the denominator's noun (`measure_noun.denominator_noun`, "GNI"), the declared plain names of the shape's slots, and the words of the label or a synonym of each code the final bindings apply that no span of that reading and no menu choice names (`phase_rank_bind._unnamed_bound_code_words`: "private" in "private grants" when the answer binds flow type 30, "private development finance", and "loans" under type of finance 420, synonym "loan instrument", while 130, which "reproductive health" names, leaves "policy" in "reproductive health and education policy ODA" unread); the shape's own label is excluded from the readers. On such a shape the ratio words, denominator noun words and function words of a code term read in every code system together are read too, so "ODA as a share of GNI" and "ODA GNI ratio" answer on the GNI share shape, while on an amount shape "as a share of GNI" is a record naming type of finance 2 and "GNI ratio" one naming the ODA code. The five-tuple words of a code term read in every code system together are read on every shape, and a hyphenated word is read when each of its parts is (`phase_rank_bind._holds_word`), so on the grant-equivalent shape (measured property `grantEquivalent`) "grant-equivalent ODA" and "ODA grant equivalents" (DAC2a aid type 201 "ODA grant") answer, while on an amount shape "grant-equivalent" and "grant equivalents" are records naming the ODA code, and "grants" in "ODA grants" stays a record on the grant-equivalent shape. A code span also reads the adjacent words, function words and connectives aside, that the label or a synonym of every code it names holds (`phase_rank_bind._code_span_reach`), and its neighbours lie past them, so "multilateral" beside "imputed", DAC2a aid type 106 "imputed multilateral ODA", is read, and "grants" in "imputed multilateral grants" is a record naming 106. A word of a code term read in every code system together that the shape's reading leaves unread ("multilateral" in "multilateral ODA" on a CRS shape) is such a record too, unless the name of such an unnamed bound code holds every word of it ("loans" in "ODA loans", DAC2a aid type 218, under 420). Such a run, with the code terms beside it, is a phrase the bind call is offered a bounded candidate menu for on each candidate shape (`phase_rank_bind._candidate_menus`, `CodeIndex.candidate_codes`): the codes of the shape's code systems whose labels or synonyms share a word of the run, ranked by the words they share and how rare those are, the first `CANDIDATE_HITS` (8) kept, then the codes of the terms beside it, then the ancestors and children of each, at most `CANDIDATE_LIMIT` (24) codes, less those the shape's family does not hold. A run sharing no word with any code ("maternal") has no menu, and neither has a word of a code term read in every code system together that names a code another shape the part's recall found binds (`ResolveState.shapes_found`, before a named dataset narrows them), one declaring the code's system whose family holds it: "imputed" in "imputed education aid", DAC2a aid type 106, gets no "imputed student cost" menu on a CRS shape when the imputed sectors shape is recalled, and "multilateral" in "multilateral ODA", DAC1 aid type 2000, stays an unavailable term on a part naming the CRS when a DAC1 shape holding 2000 is recalled. With no such shape the run keeps its menu, so "loans" in "ODA loans for education", DAC2a aid type 218, is offered type of finance 420, and "population", type of finance 4, keeps its menu. A shape the query's places do not bind to (`place_roles.bind_place_roles` refuses) has no menu, so "how much do African countries spend on their own health care?" offers the CRS flow shape no "general government and civil society support" for "domestic general government health", and the expenditure series answers. The menus precede bind, so the labels of bound codes read nothing for them. The bind prompt lists each menu under its phrase, every code of it whatever the 20-value taxonomy cap shows, and a term inside the phrase on a code system the menu names leaves fixed_context; a prompt with no menu is unchanged. A code the model binds that exactly one menu offers, on the code system of its binding, reads the phrase when it is a lexical hit of the menu or a descendant of one and, for each term inside the phrase on that system, is that term's code or lies under it, or has a label or synonym holding every content word of the term (`phase_rank_bind._menu_choices`, `_holds_every_word`). 41081 "environmental education and training" chosen for "environment education" reads the phrase in place of 110, though it lies under 410. 15180 chosen for "girls education" lies outside 110 and holds no "education", so it reads nothing. "education" binds 110 and "girls" keeps its record. A chosen hit reads the phrase as the furthest of its ancestors among the menu's hits whose labels or synonyms share every word of the phrase its own share (`phase_rank_bind._broadest_reading`), so 421 "standard loan" chosen for "education loans" reads as 420, whose synonym "loan instrument" shares "loans" too, and 13010 chosen for "population" reads as 130. Such a code forces in place of the terms inside the phrase on that system, combined with the other terms by the connective between them, the phrase records it with evidence `model`, and no uncovered or unavailable record falls inside the phrase. The terms' own codes, their children and the ancestors of hits read nothing and force nothing, so the terms force as they would with no menu. 121 chosen for "infant health", whose one hit is 12240, gives way to the 120 "health" names, and "infant" keeps its record. "contraceptive services ODA" binds 13030 family planning when the model chooses it from the menu its synonym "contraceptive delivery" puts it on, and the rendered sentence names it; "population policies and reproductive health" binds 130 whether the model chooses 13010 or 130 for "population policies". A phrase with no such choice keeps its terms. `SearchSettings.qualifier_coverage` (env `SEARCH_QUALIFIER_COVERAGE`, default `refuse`) decides what the records do. Under `refuse` they enter query_qualifiers and the part refuses qualifier_not_bound with the near match as flagged_match; under `log` election vetting logs each once (`qualifier_coverage would_refuse phrase=<text> codes=<dcids>`), the records stay out of query_qualifiers, and an unread word of a code term departs `measure_not_applied` with the `query_qualifier_dropped` warning. The mode enters the response cache's scope document `resolved.observations.series[].conversion`, since one Spec's series can convert under different recipes. Issue: none. No gap on default_reading: pinned by `tests/engine/test_measure_query_collapse_pipeline.py`. No gap on query_qualifiers: pinned by `tests/engine/test_qualifier_trace.py` and, for the disposition and coverage rules, `tests/engine/test_qualifier_guard.py`; the coverage mode's cache key by `tests/engine/test_response_cache_keys.py`. | C103   | diagnostics carries engine_build, trace_id, warnings, timing_ms, timed_out; a part the request's soft deadline leaves unanswered is a `no_data` part with reason `timed_out` (category `unresolved`), whose sentence says the time limit was reached                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | implemented |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| C179   | The response cache holds complete answers. A buffered answer is its response, with or without `include_observations` or `include_option_coverage`. A request whose transport streams trailing events (SSE with either flag) stores its events from `response` to `done` when `done` is sent, apart from `place_options` events; an identical streamed request replays them between a fresh `response` and `done`, keeps the stored `done` warnings and late departures, sends no `step`, `interpretation` or `sentence` event, and streams the place options a fresh run streams after the replayed events, under the same budget, from the option-set cache when it holds them. A streamed entry and a buffered one never serve each other. A stream that ends before `done` stores nothing, and neither does one whose `done` timed out or carries an unstorable warning, or one that dropped a slot-options or observations event to a failure or deadline. Place options never block storage. Layer 1 keys a `raw_text` question on its normalised text; layer 2 keys it on the extraction output and a `parsed` request on its whole input. Each key adds the request options, the engine fingerprint, the UTC day, the graph generation, the coverage stamp, the conversion catalogue revision, and the origin catalogue's `build_id` and `un_system_enabled`. A response with a timeout, a degraded fan-out leg, a read lost to a graph or SDMX failure, a decision that consulted a default place scope the request alone took as a countries fallback, or any of the warnings `skills_index_unavailable`, `skills_index_incomplete`, `un_system_unavailable` naming failed reads in `params.variables`, carrying no `params`, or naming a refused place outside the origin catalogue build's refused set (C107a), `membership_unverified`, `role_declaration_unverified`, `unit_conversion_unavailable`, `option_coverage_probe_failed`, `option_coverage_timed_out`, `availability_probe_failed`, `observations_probe_failed`, `observations_timed_out`, `observations_bulk_fetch_failed` and `alternates_unevaluated` is not stored; `un_system_unavailable` naming only places in the build's refused set is stored with the answer. An availability verdict left unknown because no read covered every place the part names, with no read lost (a one-shape menu makes no read over a named set's places), marks nothing, and the answer is stored. Settings `SEARCH_RESPONSE_CACHE_MAX_BYTES` (64 MiB) and `SEARCH_RESPONSE_CACHE_MAX_ENTRY_BYTES` (2 MiB) bound it, an answer held under both layers' keys counted once; an answer over the entry bound is served and not kept | implemented |     |
| C180   | A `dataset_resubmit` request serves each `identity` entry from a per-part memo. The key is the canonical JSON of the whole `ResubmitIdentityPayload` (the identity and its `entity_groups`), the four enrichment flags, the build commit, the graph generation, the coverage stamp, the conversion catalogue revision, the origin catalogue's `build_id` and `un_system_enabled`, and the UTC day. A region is stored before its trailing work starts, holding no task or closure; a hit rebuilds the closures from the current request, so a streamed hit still emits its trailing `observations` event. A region with a lost read, an unsettled default scope, an alternate left unevaluated or an unstorable warning (C179) is not stored. Settings `SEARCH_RESPONSE_CACHE_MEMO_MAX_BYTES` (64 MiB; 0 turns the memo off) and `SEARCH_RESPONSE_CACHE_MEMO_MAX_ENTRY_BYTES` (8 MiB) bound it, each region weighed once, when stored, by the heap it holds, with the process-wide code, measure and coverage indexes counted as shared; a region over the entry bound is served and not kept                                                                 | implemented |     |
| C181   | Each resolve whose body starts ends with one INFO log line, written by the route outside the hard cap, when the last SSE frame has left or, on the buffered route, once the response is built and before it is sent, so a buffered line cannot tell whether the client received the answer. The SSE line reads delivery: a client that stalls delays the line, and one that leaves before taking the last frame reads `cancelled` on an answer the pipeline finished. A body that never starts runs no pipeline and logs no line, and neither does a refusal at the route ahead of the pipeline or a direct caller of `resolve_events`, `resolve_async` or `resolve`. The line is `resolve ` followed by a JSON object: `trace_id`, `kind`, `caller` (the User-Agent), each posted `include_*` flag, `transport` (`sse` or `buffered`), `cache` (`inactive`, `miss`, `hit layer1` or `hit layer2`), `memo_hits` and `memo_misses` (null except on `dataset_resubmit` with the memo on), `total_ms`, `by_step`, `parts_by_step` (one `by_step` per part, null for a part with no timing), `outcome` (the response's `status` for a resolution, its `kind` otherwise), `part_statuses`, `no_data_reasons`, `unsupported_codes`, `llm_usage` (`diagnostics.llm_usage`), `error_code`, `timed_out`, `degraded_leg` and `query_hmac` (the HMAC-SHA-256 of the casefolded normalised query under `SEARCH_QUERY_HMAC_KEY`, null except on `raw_text` and null with the key unset). `query_hmac` counts repeats within a deployment: digests do not compare across deployments, guessing a query offline needs the key, and rotating the key breaks repeat counts across the rotation. `error_code` is null for a request that ran to its end, otherwise the problem code served for a failure or refusal inside the pipeline, or `cancelled` for a cancellation the hard cap did not cause. `timeout` covers the hard cap and any `TimeoutError` that fails the request. Nothing ends an SSE request once its `done` frame has left, and a disconnect before then reads `cancelled`; on the buffered route a failure or the hard cap after `done` still reads here. `timed_out` is true for a `timeout`, else the `done` event's flag, null before `done`; `degraded_leg` is the `done` event's flag, null before `done`. Timing, `outcome` and `llm_usage` are null, and the part lists empty, when the request ended before its response event. The line carries no query text | implemented |     |

An "all" phrase is met by one series per member or by the total over them; an "each" or "every" phrase asks for the breakdown (`role_text.every_member_breakdown`), so only an answer declaring a role on the axis meets it. ### Refusal code detail (per C112)

This table lists every code carrying a registry mapping on a `problem_details` or `unsupported_code` channel, whether or not this deployment's own doors raise it yet. `role_signature_incompatible` is exactly that case on its `search.resolve` mapping: registered against the resolve door with an outstanding raise site there. A code minted only on a `no_data_reason` channel, with no HTTP mapping anywhere in its registry row (`no_observations`, `qualifier_not_bound`, and the rest of NoDataReason's own pure members), carries no row here; it sits in the no_data reasons enumerated by C50/C50a.

## Limits

| Clause | Requirement                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Status      | Gap |
| ------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------- | --- |
| C115   | raw_text.query: at most 2,000 characters, refuses query_too_long 422 over the limit                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | implemented |     |
| C116   | parsed field caps are published in the capability document; violation refuses request_invalid                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | implemented |     |
| C117   | The candidates cap per part is a server setting, `max_candidates` (default 6, at least 2 in effect), published in the capability document; `response_options` has no such field                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | implemented |     |
| C118   | Observation preview caps (points-per-series, series count, and `role_bulk_rank_max_rows`, the rows a role-bearing bulk preview may read whole and rank) are published in the capability document                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | implemented |     |
| C128   | Role-scope discovery's own open-axis member/variable/source reads are local lookups over the coverage index the bound graph release serves, not SDMX reads, so they carry no member cap of their own; the capability document publishes the year floor and window-length cap that bound this discovery (`scope_min_year`, `scope_max_window_years`), and separately the one SDMX-bounded member cap elsewhere in Search (`region_max_members` for region resolution's alt-shape pre-warm; the options door's own observationAbout facet-membership probe carries no StatVar-count precheck of its own) | implemented |     |
| C146   | Every role-bearing resolved Spec carries an exact `headline` (observations, series, and per-role dcid counts in one year, and whether the request itself named a year). For a `latest` window the year is the one its variables elect: of the three calendar years ending at the newest year a variable's series hold, at or before the year the coverage artifact was built, the newest that at least half as many series hold as the fullest of the three, and with several variables the newest year any of them elects. For a named window it is the latest year any served series has data inside the window. `recent_years` holds the counts for three calendar years, newest first, with a year that has no data kept at zero and no year before the coverage artifact's epoch: for a `latest` window the raw counts over every series the answer holds, ending at the newest year any holds at or before the artifact's build year, and for a named window the counts for the headline year and the two before it. The headline is computed from the coverage artifact, alongside a `coverage_stamp` naming the artifact version it was confirmed against; both are `null` for a non-role-bearing Spec                                                                                                                                                   | implemented |                                                   |
| C119   | All returned lists are capped with a truncation flag; there is no pagination                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | implemented |     |
| C182   | A resubmit whose member walk (C15) enters the full read of the filtered set, up to `SEARCH_RESUBMIT_SUBJECT_CEILING` (20,000) subjects, the ceiling the Datasets mint shares so the two read one cached family, has a request deadline of `SEARCH_RESUBMIT_FULL_READ_TIMEOUT_S` (90 s) from the request's start. Every other dataset_resubmit and every other door keeps `SEARCH_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_S` (45 s). A buffered dataset_resubmit response waits up to 90 s, and a streamed response sends a heartbeat comment every 10 s so the connection stays open. A client's own timeout must allow 90 s for a dataset_resubmit | implemented | Pinned by `tests/engine/test_streaming.py` and `tests/engine/test_entry_rate_cache.py`. |

## Open gaps

Grouped by root cause. **Issue: none** marks gaps with no open tracking issue.

| Gap                              | Affects                                       | What's needed                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | Issue    |
| -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------- |
| Identity hashes graph state      | C73                                           | Freeze each shape's slot-key taxonomy independent of the queried place, so spec_ids never rotate on a graph refresh.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | #177     |
| Role-keyed retrieval (live data) | C51 (live verification), resolution_ambiguous | Role-keyed retrieval is generic. A shape whose members declare non-subject entity roles resolves through the role-keyed SDMX path under the names those members declare. It runs against fixtures. Every resolution door routes a role-bearing shape through this same path, including a base/promote posting that reconfirms one. A directional resubmit carries both axes in its nested identity: `entities.destination` and `entities.origin`. A role named in neither `entities` nor `open_roles` is the total over its members: a question that names no recipient or creditor reads the series published without one, and a member declaring a role the question leaves out answers only where no member with fewer roles does. Members with fewer roles displace it only where the constraint properties of each of them are among those of every member they displace: a recipient-only series that breaks down by a property a by-creditor series does not hold is the total of none. A role in `open_roles` joins the election only when a member the bound roles alone elect, or a member at the same constraint values (its role twin, such as a by-creditor series beside its total), declares it; otherwise the question is read as if the role were unnamed. Members whose role sets do not nest, and members whose role sets nest across other constraint properties, refuse `role_signature_incompatible`. Posting `identity.entities.origin` onto a standard shape refuses `role_incompatible` (422, pointer `/input/identity/entities/origin`), since a standard shape's resolved StatVars declare no directional role vocabulary. Live traffic waits on role-declaring data landing in the graph. The presentation vocabulary (`EntityRoleKey`) is closed at the subject and the two axis names in use, so a declared third role resolves and filters, and the presentation map does not expose it. | none |
| raw_text unit wiring             | C97, C99, C101, C102, part of C107            | raw_text extraction must populate `unit_text` so the shared binder is reachable from both text doors.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | #103     |
| Entity confidence signal         | C56, C77                                      | An entity-resolution confidence source in the graph client; the wire field exists but is always null.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | none     |
| Option-list metadata             | C91                                           | `entity_type` on option items; a graph-revision stamp once a revision source exists for search's graph path.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | none     |
| no_data order enforcement        | C51                                           | Structural enforcement of `NO_DATA_RESOLUTION_ORDER` across phases. Each election runs through `elect_no_data_reason`, but the phases return at their first refusal, so a check reordered against the order answers the earlier phase's reason.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | none     |
| Sentence axis storage            | C68                                           | Derive a slot's sentence axis from the shape grammar rather than storing it per-slot.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | none     |
| Observation facet fan-out        | C82                                           | Decide: fan series out per facet tuple, or renegotiate the clause to the deliberate one-facet-per-series design.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | none     |
| Per-series truncation fields     | C86                                           | `series_truncated`/`total_series` on the observation preview body.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | none     |
